I recall a common solution for 24 with numbers like 1, 3, 4, 6 is something like 6/(1-3/4)=6/(1-0.75)=6/0.25=24. That is a well-known trick: a/(b - c/d). For our numbers, can we mimic that structure? We want something like X/(Y - Z/W) = 24. We need X, Y, Z, W to be our numbers 1, 2, 9, 11 in some order.

Let's try: 11/(9 - 2/1) = 11/(9-2)=11/7, no.
11/(2 - 9/1)=11/(2-9)=11/ -7, no.
9/(11 - 2/1)=9/(11-2)=9/9=1, no.
9/(2 - 11/1)=9/(2-11)=9/ -9= -1, no.
2/(11 - 9/1)=2/(11-9)=2/2=1, no.

1/(11 - 9/2)=1/(11-4.5)=1/6.5, no.
1/(9 - 11/2)=1/(9-5.5)=1/3.5, no.
11/(1 - 9/2)=11/(1-4.5)=11/ -3.5= -22/7, no.
11/(1 - 2/9)=11/(1-0.2222)=11/0.7778=14.1429, no.
9/(1 - 11/2)=9/(1-5.5)=9/ -4.5= -2, no.
9/(1 - 2/11)=9/(1-0.1818)=9/0.8182=11, no.
2/(1 - 9/11)=2/(1-0.81818)=2/0.18182=11, no.
1/(1 - 9/11)=1/(1-0.81818)=1/0.18182=5.5, no.
1/(1 - 11/9)=1/(1-1.2222)=1/ -0.2222= -4.5, no.
